# [CI] Add check-docstring-style pre-commit hook and use single backticks in docstrings

[Upstream PR](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65158) · state: open · merged: False

## Original PR description

<!-- markdownlint-disable -->

## Overview

Add a `check-docstring-style` pre-commit hook for docstring Markdown style and apply it to `vllm/`. Follow-up to #65157, the docs fix it guards.

## Claims

- Flags `[a][b]` in rendered docstrings (module, class, function, method and attribute docstrings, including under `if`/`try`/`with`) unless the target is fully qualified (`[LLM][vllm.LLM]`) or a collapsed identifier (`[int][]`), since mkdocs-autorefs reads it as a cross-reference.
- Rewrites ` ``code`` ` as `` `code` `` in docstrings, pairing backtick runs per Markdown paragraph as Python-Markdown does.
- Code blocks (fenced, indented relative to Google section content, doctest) are skipped, and so are docstrings whose source lines differ from their value (implicit concatenation, escapes). CRLF and BOM are preserved. It covers all of `vllm/` except vendored code, which the global exclude already skips.
- One output line per failure: `path:line: message`.
- Commits after the hook: the backtick rewrite, then a rewrap of paragraphs it shortened where that saves lines (1,122 files in total, -1,206 net lines).
- `AGENTS.md` gets one line: single backticks for code in docstrings, enforced by the hook.

## Validation

| Check | Result |
| --- | --- |
| Cross-ref rule on the #65157 files before that fix | all 11 reported warnings flagged |
| Rewrite + rewrap vs `upstream/main`: code with docstrings blanked | identical in every changed file |
| Rewrite + rewrap: each changed docstring parsed with griffe (Google) and rendered with Python-Markdown | same HTML in all 1,122 files (except the intended `[block0] [block1]` fix in `_custom_ops.py`) |
| Hook over the hooked `vllm/**/*.py` again | no rewrites, no findings |
| Edge cases from an independent review (wrapped spans, CRLF, BOM, form feeds, concatenation, nested/escaped/spaced links, sections, 4-backtick fences, tabs) | pass |

```bash
pre-commit run check-docstring-style --all-files   # Passed
pre-commit run                                     # all hooks pass
```

Not a duplicate: no open PR adds docstring style checks. Regenerate the rewrite on fresh main with `pre-commit run check-docstring-style --all-files`.
